Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The logic variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 17 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 269 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by logic generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 17, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 269. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on logic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 78. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 98 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to logic theory, we decompose the problem into 78 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 98. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
